# Agents · R · part 3

905 entries, rcode-hussain-pm to react-security. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **rcode-hussain-pm**: Product Manager — for PRD, user-story drafting, acceptance criteria, scope definition, MoSCoW / RICE prioritization, sprint planning, backlog curation, JTBD framing. Activates: PRD writing, "what should v1 include", "split this story", "is this in scope", "talk to Hussain-PM", PM review. Do NOT use for: technical feasibility (Waleed), implementation (Hanzla / Yousef / Haitham), market positioning (Mariam), strategic go/no-go and kill criteria (Sadiq), QA test strategy (Fatima), sprint scrum ops (Hussain-SM). - **rd-barb-state**: Seeded from Barb's state-file structure. Only rd-barb writes here; everyone else reads. ([D-sorganization/Runner_Dashboard](https://github.com/D-sorganization/Runner_Dashboard/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/rd-barb-state.md))
- **rd-critic**: Independent critic for the Golden Dragon (Red Dragon brain). Give it the owner's request plus EITHER the main agent's written reasoning/plan (mode review) OR only the deliverable paths and the request, without the main agent's conclusions (mode verify). It returns one JSON verdict - ok, fix or better - with at most 3 concrete issues and a corrected step. Use it for L2/L3 work before expensive or hard-to-undo steps, and before delivering anything costly. Read-only: it never edits, runs, publishes, buys or deletes. ([kosif199022-jpg/black-dragon-skill](https://github.com/kosif199022-jpg/black-dragon-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/rd-critic.md) · 2 copies)
- **rd-director**: Director of R&D who owns technical strategy, the portfolio, stage-gate GO / NO-GO decisions (concept, feasibility, design freeze, pilot, release), technical risk appetite, build-versus-buy and resourcing calls. Read-only. Use when a gate needs a verdict, a build-versus-buy or resourcing call is due, or two departments conflict. Not for building the schedule or risk register (senior-project-manager) or reviewing one change (reviewer). ([srinator22/agent-kernel](https://github.com/srinator22/agent-kernel/blob/HEAD/docs/roster/agents/rd-director.md))
- **rd-engineer**: Use this agent when the user types /rd or asks for r&d engineer work — e.g., spike on whether we can replace pgvector with Lance. The agent covers prototyping, spike branches, feasibility studies, tech radar. Examples — &lt;example&gt;user "spike on whether we can replace pgvector with Lance" → Reactor produces a recommendation in the standard 5-options + ⭐ pick format and references project conventions surfaced from bullpen-memory.&lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;user "/rd &lt;task&gt;" → direct invocation; Reactor works in their lane and hands off if the task is out of scope.&lt;/example&gt; ([Manavarya09/bullpen](https://github.com/Manavarya09/bullpen/blob/HEAD/agents/rd-engineer.md) · ★11)
- **rd-feasibility**: 資深 RD 可行性評估者。從 PRD 產出概念資料模型、整合點、技術風險與 NFR 初判。/feasibility 會派出此 agent。 ([chu1999tw/specrail](https://github.com/chu1999tw/specrail/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/rd-feasibility.md))

Use it for mechanical steps in a Red Dragon plan marked 🤖 rd-runner. - **re-lifecycle**: Lifecycle stance: allocation, free, ownership, initialisation and error paths. High precision, cheap to run. ([tihanyin/REx-skill](https://github.com/tihanyin/REx-skill/blob/HEAD/claude-skill/agents/re-lifecycle.md) · ★125)
- **re-listener**: Lists every input a game reads from outside: controls, clocks and timers, random seeds, files and configuration, hardware detection, window messages and network messages, with the address of each read, and proposes the port's input record. - **re-logic**: Logic and trust stance: authorisation, state machines, crypto choices, validate-here-use-there. No signature to grep for. ([tihanyin/REx-skill](https://github.com/tihanyin/REx-skill/blob/HEAD/claude-skill/agents/re-logic.md) · ★125)
- **re-mapper**: Maps how a game uses its machine: hardware registers and ports, memory banks and overlays, video and sound chips, and the variables in RAM, with a name and evidence for each address. Use for DOS, home computer, console and arcade games before tracing their logic. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/agents/re-mapper.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-matcher**: Checks whether a ready implementation matches the game's own version: a GameFrame crate (OPL2, WMA, MSVC CRT, Turbo Pascal), a released engine source or a library, by running the original on many inputs and comparing bit for bit. - **re-measurer**: Runs chosen functions of the original under the Unicorn emulator (Windows x64, 32-bit x86 Windows, or 16-bit DOS/NE) to capture exact ground truth for chosen inputs, and records the calls they make, such as random draws. Use for anything a reimplementation must match bit for bit. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/re-measurer.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-observer**: Runs the original game where it can be watched, driven and captured (GameFrame's emulators, DOSBox-X, 86Box, Wine, a streamed PC) and records frames, state, inputs, file reads and sound as measured evidence; lists every input and screen. - **re-planner**: Reverse-engineering intake analyst AND modeling strategist. Views part photos and rendered STL screenshots, analyzes the scan with trimesh, and produces the INTAKE CARD, the caliper MEASUREMENT REQUEST, and the ordered MODELING PLAN with a FEATURE BUDGET. First phase of every STL→STEP part in the OOMWOO RE line (v3). ([mikbalarikan/agentic_STL-to-CAD](https://github.com/mikbalarikan/agentic_STL-to-CAD/blob/HEAD/baseline/agents/re-planner.md) · ★1)
- **re-porter**: Reimplements traced and measured game behaviour in new code that behaves exactly like the original: same structure, numeric types, order of operations, random draw order, iteration order and integer semantics, citing the original function on every item. Use when writing the reimplementation of anything traced. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.gemini/agents/re-porter.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-publisher**: Builds and keeps the project's website in sites/NAME/ (or site/): writes a live diary entry for each milestone, consolidates each past day into one update-notes post, adds a page for each new document through @include, keeps the front page's status true, and checks that nothing from the game is on it. Use after a milestone, a release, or when the documents in docs/ change shape. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/agents/re-publisher.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-qa-verifier**: Independent reverse-engineering QA gate (v3). Re-measures the rebuilt STEP against scan, calipers, and photos with its own registration (never trusting drafter numbers), audits Tier-0 surface quality, and writes an APPROVED/REVISE verdict into the part's repo folder. Mandatory final gate — feature-level review never replaces it. ([mikbalarikan/agentic_STL-to-CAD](https://github.com/mikbalarikan/agentic_STL-to-CAD/blob/HEAD/baseline/agents/re-qa-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **re-randomiser**: Finds every random number generator in a game, its state, its seeding and every caller, then measures the draw order with call traces so a port can draw the same numbers in the same order. Use before porting anything that draws random numbers. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/re-randomiser.agent.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-reader**: Read-only reverse-engineering reader for Def Jam recomp. Give it a named function address or file and a bounded question about the lifted C, the XBE image or the docs; it reports what the code does and writes docs/research/&lt;topic&gt;.md. It never edits code, builds or runs the game. ([sweeax-exe/ffny-online](https://github.com/sweeax-exe/ffny-online/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/re-reader.md) · ★31)
- **re-recon**: Phase 1 of a binary analysis. Identifies the target, builds the threat model, extracts every artefact, and hands the next phase a map. - **re-recon**: Read-only reconnaissance for a binary/CTF/malware target. Spawn this when recon means reading large dumps (full strings, disassembly, many source files) — it does the breadth-first sweep and returns a tight triage summary + recommended path, keeping the raw output out of the caller's context. - **re-reconcile**: Final phase. Adjudicates the stance agents' findings against the code, produces the single reconciled deliverable and the written report. - **re-refuter**: Adversarial checker. - **re-reviewer**: Station 3.2 Fix-Round Auditor. - **re-scout**: Read-only scout for the PS2 OSD. - **re-typer**: Recovers the game's structures, classes and enumerations from the code that uses them: sizes from allocations, fields from constructors, serialisers and every access, the C++ containers they hold, and names from evidence, written to Ghidra and labels/fields.tsv. - **re-verifier**: Proves that a reimplementation matches the original: bit-exact parity against emulator ground truth, random-draw order, determinism with digests, recordings and replays, real save files, pictures against the original. - **re-verifier**: Independent re-audit of a fix. MUST BE USED after test-engineer completes a fix. Cannot approve its own team's work -- must either CONFIRM or REJECT with evidence. - **re-voicer**: Content-preserving re-voicing utility. Re-renders supplied text (review findings, a summary, notes — any output) in a selected voice/persona WITHOUT changing its meaning. Pass the source text and a voice name (e.g. "gentry"). Returns the re-voiced text only. OUTPUT RELAY RULE: the re-voiced prose IS the product. Pass the agent's COMPLETE output to the user VERBATIM — do not summarize, condense, paraphrase, or reformat. Summarizing destroys the value. ([enzuguri/understudy](https://github.com/enzuguri/understudy/blob/HEAD/agents/re-voicer.md))
- **re4-orchestrator**: Team orchestrator of the RE4 port. Runs one team's queue (issue files → Sonnet workers → port-reviewer → integrate) in the main checkout and reports to the command center.
